To address this limitation, we propose Optical Context Retrieval Memory (OCR-Memory), a memory framework that leverages the visual modality as a high-density representation of agent experience, enabling retention of arbitrarily long histories with minimal prompt overhead at retrieval time. Specifically, OCR-Memory renders historical trajectories into images annotated with unique visual identifiers. OCR-Memory retrieves stored experience via a \emph{locate-and-transcribe} paradigm that selects relevant regions through visual anchors and retrieves the corresponding verbatim text, avoiding free-form generation and reducing hallucination. Experiments on long-horizon agent benchmarks show consistent gains under strict context limits, demonstrating that optical encoding increases effective memory capacity while preserving faithful evidence recovery.

</details>


### [112] [SAGE: A Strategy-Aware Graph-Enhanced Generation Framework For Online Counseling](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.26630)
*Eliya Naomi Aharon,Meytal Grimland,Avi Segal,Loona Ben Dayan,Inbar Shenfeld,Yossi Levi Belz,Kobi Gal*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: SAGE是一个将结构化临床知识与生成式AI相结合的框架，通过异构图构建、策略分类器和图感知注意力机制，提升大语言模型在心理健康咨询中的临床推理能力，在策略预测和响应质量上优于基线模型。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 通用大语言模型在心理健康咨询中缺乏将心理学理论框架、实时痛苦信号和策略性干预计划相整合的临床推理能力，这对安全性和治疗效果至关重要。

Method: 构建异构图统一对话动态与心理学理论层；使用Next Strategy Classifier预测最优治疗干预策略；通过Graph-Aware Attention机制将图结构信号投影为软提示，引导LLM生成具有临床深度的回复。

Result: 在自动评估指标和专家人工评估中，SAGE在策略预测和推荐响应质量上均优于基线模型。

Conclusion: SAGE可作为高危机心理咨询中的决策支持工具，通过提供可操作的干预建议来增强人类专家的能力。

Abstract: Effective mental health counseling is a complex, theory-driven process requiring the simultaneous integration of psychological frameworks, real-time distress signals, and strategic intervention planning. This level of clinical reasoning is critical for safety and therapeutic effectiveness but is often missing in general-purpose Large Language Models (LLMs). We introduce SAGE (Strategy-Aware Graph-Enhanced), a novel framework designed to bridge the gap between structured clinical knowledge and generative AI. SAGE constructs a heterogeneous graph that unifies conversational dynamics with a psychologically grounded layer, explicitly anchoring interactions in a theory-driven lexicon. Our architecture first employs a Next Strategy Classifier to identify the optimal therapeutic intervention. Subsequently, a Graph-Aware Attention mechanism projects graph-derived structural signals into soft prompts, conditioning the LLM to generate responses that maintain clinical depth. Validated through both automated metrics and expert human evaluation, SAGE outperforms baselines in strategy prediction and recommended response quality. By providing actionable intervention recommendations, SAGE serves as a cutting-edge decision-support tool designed to augment human expertise in high-stakes crisis counseling.

</details>


### [113] [Differentially-Private Text Rewriting reshapes Linguistic Style](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.26656)
*Stefan Arnold*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 本文研究了差分隐私文本重写对文本语域特征的影响，发现隐私保护会导致文本的互动标记、语境参照和复杂从属结构严重流失，使不同文本趋同于非参与性和非说服性语域，造成风格同质化。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 差分隐私文本改写技术虽能平衡隐私保护与语法连贯性，但其对文本语域身份（register identity）的影响尚未被充分探索。

Method: 对差分隐私重写文本进行多维度风格特征分析，比较自回归释义与双向替换两种架构在不同隐私预算下的表现。

Result: 隐私约束下的重写会导致互动标记、语境参照和复杂从属结构严重流失；两种架构均迫使文本趋同于非参与性和非说服性语域。

Conclusion: 差分隐私文本重写在保护语义内容的同时，会系统性同质化人类文本中细微的风格标记，抹除作者的语域身份特征。

Abstract: Differential Privacy (DP) for text matured from disjointed word-level substitutions to contiguous sentence-level rewriting by leveraging the generative capacity of language models. While this form of text privatization is best suited for balancing formal privacy guarantees with grammatical coherence, its impact on the register identity of text remains largely unexplored. By conducting a multidimensional stylistic profiling of differentially-private rewriting, we demonstrate that the cost of privacy extends far beyond lexical variation. Specifically, we find that rewriting under privacy constraints induces a systematic functional mutation of the text's communicative signature. This shift is characterized by the severe attrition of interactive markers, contextual references, and complex subordination. By comparing autoregressive paraphrasing against bidirectional substitution across a spectrum of privacy budgets, we observe that both architectures force convergence toward a non-involved and non-persuasive register. This register-blind sanitization effectively preserves semantic content but structurally homogenizes the nuanced stylistic markers that define human-authored discourse.

</details>


### [114] [From Black-Box Confidence to Measurable Trust in Clinical AI: A Framework for Evidence, Supervision, and Staged Autonomy](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.26671)
*Serhii Zabolotnii,Viktoriia Holinko,Olha Antonenko*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种基于证据、监督和分阶段自主三原则的可信临床AI框架，通过确定性核心、患者特定AI助手、多模型分级机制和人工监督层的架构设计，并引入基于计量学原理的信任度量体系，将可信赖性构建为系统架构属性而非单一模型特征。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 临床AI的信任不能简化为模型准确率、生成流畅度或用户印象，需要在医学领域将信任工程化为可测量的系统属性，建立基于证据、监督和自主性操作边界的可信框架。

Method: 提出三原则框架：(1)证据层：保留确定性核心逻辑，结合患者特定AI助手进行上下文验证；(2)监督层：人工验证、升级和风险控制机制；(3)分阶段自主层：多模型分级升级机制。采用分类器驱动的模块化提示方法实现渐进式临床深度扩展，并基于计量学原理（测量不确定度、校准、可追溯性）构建信任度量体系。

Result: 证明信任还取决于对临床关键发现的选择性验证、有界临床上下文、严谨的提示架构以及在真实案例上的仔细评估。分类器驱动的模块化提示可作为在不牺牲提示性能的前提下渐进扩展临床深度的路径。

Conclusion: 可信临床AI不是单个模型的属性，而是一种架构设计的结果——从系统设计之初就嵌入证据追踪、人工监督、分级升级和渐进式行动权限。

Abstract: Trust in clinical artificial intelligence (AI) cannot be reduced to model accuracy, fluency of generation, or overall positive user impression. In medicine, trust must be engineered as a measurable system property grounded in evidence, supervision, and operational boundaries of AI autonomy. This article proposes a practical framework for trustworthy clinical AI built around three principles: evidence, supervision, and staged autonomy. Rather than replacing deterministic clinical logic wholesale with end-to-end black-box models, the proposed approach combines a deterministic core, a patient-specific AI assistant for contextual validation, a multi-tier model escalation mechanism, and a human supervision layer for verification, escalation, and risk control. We demonstrate that trust also depends on selective verification of clinically critical findings, bounded clinical context, disciplined prompt architecture, and careful evaluation on realistic cases. Classifier-driven modular prompting is examined as an incremental path to scaling clinical depth without sacrificing prompt performance and without waiting for complete rule-based coverage. To operationalize trust, a set of trust metrics is proposed, built on metrological principles -- measurement uncertainty, calibration, traceability -- enabling quantitative rather than subjective assessment of each architectural layer. In this perspective, trustworthy clinical AI emerges not as a property of an individual model, but as an architectural outcome of a system into which evidence trails, human oversight, tiered escalation, and graduated action rights are embedded from the outset.

</details>


### [115] [Swap distance minimization shapes the order of subject, object and verb in languages of the world](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.26726)
*Jairo Rios-El-Yazidi,Ramon Ferrer-i-Cancho*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 本文发现，即使在非SOV/SVO主导语序或缺乏主导语序的语言中，语序变异仍然遵循交换距离最小化原则。这一规律跨语言家族和大区域普遍存在。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有研究主要关注SOV和SVO这两种最常见的语序，但对其他语序类型（如VSO、VOS、OSV、OVS）及缺乏主导语序的语言关注不足。本研究旨在检验交换距离最小化原则是否在更广泛的语序类型中普遍成立。

Method: 跨语言家族和大区域分析，考察不同语言中主语、宾语、动词的语序变异，检验语序变化是否遵循交换距离最小化（即最小化语序排列之间的转换步数）。

Result: 交换距离最小化原则不仅适用于SOV/SVO主导语序的语言，也适用于其他主导语序（如VSO等）以及缺乏单一主导语序的语言，且这一规律在不同语言家族和地理区域中均成立。

Conclusion: 交换距离最小化是跨语言普遍存在的语序变异约束原则，其适用范围比以往认为的更广，不局限于特定主导语序类型。

Abstract: Languages of the world vary concerning the order of subject, object and verb. The most frequent dominant orders are SOV and SVO, and researchers have tailored models to this fact. However, there are still languages whose dominant order does not conform to these expectations or even lack a dominant order. Here we show that across linguistic families and macroareas, word order variation within languages is shaped by the principle of swap distance minimization even when the dominant order is not SOV/SVO and even when a dominant order is lacking.

</details>


### [116] [Domain-Adapted Small Language Models for Reliable Clinical Triage](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.26766)
*Manar Aljohani,Brandon Ho,Kenneth McKinley,Dennis Ren,Xuan Wang*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 本研究评估了开源小语言模型（SLMs）在急诊分诊中作为隐私保护型决策支持工具的可行性。通过对多种SLM和提示流水线的系统比较，发现临床小插曲（分诊叙述的简洁摘要）能产生最准确的预测。Qwen2.5-7B在准确性、稳定性和计算效率间取得最佳平衡。通过大规模领域自适应微调（使用专家审核和银标准儿科分诊数据），微调后的Qwen2.5-7B显著减少了分诊不一致性和临床重大错误，超越了所有基线SLM和先进的专有大语言模型（如GPT-4o）。这些发现强调了针对机构特定SLM进行定向微调的重要性，而非依赖更复杂的推理策略。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 急诊科中ESI分诊的准确性和一致性长期存在问题，高度可变的自由文本分诊记录导致误分诊和工作流程低效；同时需要隐私保护的解决方案。

Method: 系统比较了多种SLM和不同提示流水线；采用临床小插曲作为输入形式；使用专家审核和银标准儿科分诊数据进行大规模领域自适应微调Qwen2.5-7B。

Result: Qwen2.5-7B在准确性、稳定性和计算效率间表现最佳；微调后的模型显著降低了分诊不一致性和临床重大错误，优于所有基线SLM和GPT-4o等专有LLM。

Conclusion: 机构特定SLM可以作为可靠、隐私保护的ESI决策支持工具；定向微调比复杂的推理策略更为重要。

Abstract: Accurate and consistent Emergency Severity Index (ESI) assignment remains a persistent challenge in emergency departments, where highly variable free-text triage documentation contributes to mistriage and workflow inefficiencies. This study evaluates whether open-source small language models (SLMs) can serve as reliable, privacy-preserving decision-support tools for clinical triage. We systematically compared multiple SLMs across diverse prompting pipelines and found that clinical vignettes, concise summaries of triage narratives, yielded the most accurate predictions. The SLM, Qwen2.5-7B, demonstrated the strongest balance of accuracy, stability, and computational efficiency. Through large-scale domain adaptation using expert-curated and silver-standard pediatric triage data, fine-tuned Qwen2.5-7B models substantially reduced discordance and clinically significant errors, outperforming all baseline SLMs and advanced proprietary large language models (LLMs, e.g., GPT-4o). These findings highlight the feasibility of institution-specific SLMs for reliable, privacy-preserving ESI decision support and underscore the importance of targeted fine-tuning over more complex inference strategies.

</details>


### [117] [Decoupling Knowledge and Task Subspaces for Composable Parametric Retrieval Augmented Generation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.26768)
*Weihang Su,Hanwen Zhang,Qingyao Ai,Yiqun Liu*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 本文提出正交子空间分解（OSD）方法，通过将可复用的任务行为与文档特定知识分离到正交子空间中训练适配器，以解决参数化检索增强生成（PRAG）中多适配器合并时任务行为累积导致的不稳定问题，实验表明该策略能提升多文档PRAG的组合鲁棒性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: PRAG中采用任务监督目标训练文档适配器会导致文档特定事实与可复用任务行为纠缠在一起，当推理时合并多个适配器时，重叠的任务行为会累积，使合并后的适配器不稳定且偏离预期的文档知识。

Method: 提出正交子空间分解（OSD）方法：先训练一个Task LoRA捕获可复用任务行为，再在正交子空间中训练文档LoRA编码文档特定知识，从而分离任务行为与文档知识。

Result: 在多个知识密集型任务和不同模型规模上的实验表明，正交化策略能提升PRAG的组合鲁棒性，尤其在合并多个文档适配器时效果显著。

Conclusion: 正交子空间分解是一种有效的PRAG适配器训练方法，通过分离任务行为与文档知识，能够改善多适配器合并时的稳定性和知识聚焦能力。

Abstract: Parametric Retrieval-Augmented Generation (PRAG) encodes external documents into lightweight parameter modules that can be retrieved and merged at inference time, offering a promising alternative to in-context retrieval augmentation. Despite its potential, many PRAG implementations train document adapters with task-supervised objectives, which may cause each adapter to encode both document-specific facts and reusable task-solving behavior. This entanglement may make adapter composition less reliable: when multiple adapters are merged at inference time, their overlapping task behaviors can accumulate together with document-specific updates, potentially making the merged adapter less stable and less focused on the intended document knowledge. To examine this issue, we explore Orthogonal Subspace Decomposition (OSD), an adapter-training setup that separates reusable task behavior from document-specific knowledge adapters. Concretely, we first train a Task LoRA to capture reusable task behavior, and then train document LoRAs to encode document-specific knowledge in a orthogonal subspace. This setup provides a controlled way to examine how orthogonalizing task and document LoRA updates affects adapter composition in multi-document PRAG. Experiments across multiple knowledge-intensive tasks and model scales suggest that this orthogonalization strategy can improve compositional robustness in parametric RAG, especially when multiple document adapters are merged.

</details>


### [118] [HalluCiteChecker: A Lightweight Toolkit for Hallucinated Citation Detection and Verification in the Era of AI Scientists](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.26835)
*Yusuke Sakai,Hidetaka Kamigaito,Taro Watanabe*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: HalluCiteChecker 是一个用于检测和验证科学论文中幻觉引用的轻量级工具包，可在标准笔记本电脑上数秒内完成验证，支持离线运行和仅使用CPU，旨在减轻审稿人工作负担并支持系统化的预审和出版检查。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: AI助手技术虽提升了学术写作效率（包括引用推荐），但也导致了与任何现有工作都不对应的幻觉引用的出现，这些引用不仅损害科学论文的可信度，还增加了审稿人和作者手动验证引用的额外负担。

Method: 将幻觉引用检测形式化为一个NLP任务，并开发了对应的轻量级工具包HalluCiteChecker，该工具包可完全离线运行，仅使用CPU即可在标准笔记本电脑上数秒内完成验证。

Result: HalluCiteChecker是一个轻量级工具包，可在数秒内完成验证，支持离线运行和仅CPU运行；代码已在GitHub上以Apache 2.0许可证发布，并作为可安装包通过PyPI分发。

Conclusion: HalluCiteChecker有望帮助减轻审稿人工作负担，并支持组织者进行系统化的预审和出版检查。

Abstract: We introduce HalluCiteChecker, a toolkit for detecting and verifying hallucinated citations in scientific papers. While AI assistant technologies have transformed the academic writing process, including citation recommendation, they have also led to the emergence of hallucinated citations that do not correspond to any existing work. Such citations not only undermine the credibility of scientific papers but also impose an additional burden on reviewers and authors, who must manually verify their validity during the review process. In this study, we formalize hallucinated citation detection as an NLP task and provide a corresponding toolkit as a practical foundation for addressing this problem. Our package is lightweight and can perform verification in seconds on a standard laptop. It can also be executed entirely offline and runs efficiently using only CPUs. We hope that HalluCiteChecker will help reduce reviewer workload and support organizers by enabling systematic pre-review and publication checks. Our code is released under the Apache 2.0 license on GitHub and is distributed as an installable package via PyPI. A demonstration video is available on YouTube.

</details>


### [119] [What Kind of Language is Easy to Language-Model Under Curriculum Learning?](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.26844)
*Nadine El-Naggar,Tatsuki Kuribayashi,Ted Briscoe*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 本研究探讨了课程学习（从简单句子开始训练）如何影响语言模型的归纳偏差，进而影响其对人类语言类型学倾向（如语序偏好）的复现能力。实验发现，课程学习显著改变了语言模型展现出的归纳偏差。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 探究在何种条件下可以预测语言类型学倾向，特别是语言模型的学习偏差是否足以复现这些模式。进一步，引入学习场景这一维度，探索其与语言模型归纳偏差的交互作用。

Method: 在已有基于语言模型的类型学探索基础上，引入简单的课程学习变体（从简单句子开始训练，而非随机顺序输入），并将其作为发展动机驱动的学习场景，比较不同学习顺序下语言模型对语序等类型学特征的偏好。

Result: 课程学习显著影响了语言模型表现出的归纳偏差，即训练数据的呈现顺序会改变模型对语言类型学特征（如语序）的偏好模式。

Conclusion: 语言模型的学习场景（如课程学习）与其归纳偏差之间存在重要交互作用，这为理解语言类型学倾向的可预测性提供了新维度。

Abstract: Many of the thousands of attested languages share common configurations of features, creating a spectrum from typologically very rare (e.g., object-verb-subject word order) or impossible languages to very common combinations of features (e.g., subject-object-verb word order). One central question is under what conditions such typological tendencies can be predicted, and specifically whether the learning bias of language models (LMs) is sufficient to reproduce such patterns. In this study, we add one dimensionality to such analysis -- the learning scenario for LMs -- to explore its interaction with the inductive bias of LMs. Specifically, as a first study, we examine the effect of curriculum learning (CL), as a developmentally motivated learning scenario, i.e., starting with simpler sentences rather than randomly-ordered input. We expand existing LM-based exploration (El-Naggar et al., 2025a,b) with a simple CL variant and find that CL substantially impacts the apparent inductive bias of LMs.

</details>


### [120] [MoRFI: Monotonic Sparse Autoencoder Feature Identification](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.26866)
*Dimitris Dimakopoulos,Shay B. Cohen,Ioannis Konstas*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 论文发现大语言模型在微调过程中学习新知识会破坏其存储知识的能力，导致幻觉增加。作者提出MoRFI方法，通过稀疏自编码器识别残差流中与幻觉因果相关的潜在方向，并可通过单潜在干预恢复知识。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 后训练阶段引入的新事实常导致模型产生幻觉，但背后的机制尚不明确。论文旨在揭示有监督微调（SFT）中引入新知识如何影响模型已有知识检索，并找到导致幻觉的因果方向。

Method: 在Llama 3.1 8B、Gemma 2 9B和Mistral 7B v03上进行了受控微调实验，控制新知识比例和训练轮次。利用预训练的稀疏自编码器（SAEs）分析残差流激活，提出单调关系特征识别（MoRFI）方法，筛选对受控微调数据混合中目标属性呈单调响应的SAE特征。

Result: 增量引入新知识会增加幻觉，长时间训练会加剧此效应。MoRFI成功发现跨模型一致的因果相关潜在方向，并通过单潜在干预恢复知识。

Conclusion: 暴露于未知事实会破坏模型沿残差流中特定方向检索已有知识的能力，所提方法可可靠发现这些方向并进行干预修复。

Abstract: Large language models (LLMs) acquire most of their factual knowledge during the pre-training stage, through next token prediction. Subsequent stages of post-training often introduce new facts outwith the parametric knowledge, giving rise to hallucinations. While it has been demonstrated that supervised fine-tuning (SFT) on new knowledge may exacerbate the problem, the underlying mechanisms are still poorly understood. We conduct a controlled fine-tuning experiment, focusing on closed-book QA, and find latent directions that causally contribute to hallucinations. Specifically, we fine-tune Llama 3.1 8B, Gemma 2 9B and Mistral 7B v03 on seven distinct single QA datasets, controlling for the percentage of new knowledge and number of training epochs. By measuring performance on the test set, we validate that incrementally introducing new knowledge increases hallucinations, with the effect being more pronounced with prolonged training. We leverage pre-trained sparse autoencoders (SAEs) to analyze residual stream activations across various checkpoints for each model and propose Monotonic Relationship Feature Identification (MoRFI) for capturing causally relevant latents. MoRFI filters SAE features that respond monotonically to controlled fine-tuning data mixtures of a target property. Our findings show that exposure to unknown facts disrupts the model's ability to retrieve stored knowledge along a set of directions in the residual stream. Our pipeline reliably discovers them across distinct models, recovering knowledge through single-latent interventions.

</details>


### [121] [HealthNLP_Retrievers at ArchEHR-QA 2026: Cascaded LLM Pipeline for Grounded Clinical Question Answering](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.26880)
*Md Biplob Hosen,Md Alomgeer Hussein,Md Akmol Masud,Omar Faruque,Tera L Reynolds,Lujie Karen Chen*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 本文介绍了HealthNLP_Retrievers团队在ArchEHR-QA 2026共享任务中开发的系统，采用基于Gemini 2.5 Pro大语言模型的多阶段级联流水线，通过四个集成模块（查询重构、启发式证据评分、接地响应生成、高精度多对多对齐）处理EHR中的患者问答任务，在多个赛道中取得了具有竞争力的排名。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 患者门户虽使个体能够直接访问电子健康记录，但仅提供访问权限并不能确保患者理解或处理其中的复杂临床信息，因此需要开发能够基于EHR进行接地问答的系统来弥合这一鸿沟。

Method: 提出多阶段级联流水线架构，包含四个集成模块：(1) 少样本查询重构单元，对冗长患者查询进行摘要；(2) 基于启发式的证据评分器，对临床句子排序以优先保证召回率；(3) 接地响应生成器，严格限定在已识别证据内合成专业级回答；(4) 高精度多对多对齐框架，将生成答案链接到支持性临床句子。该流水线由Gemini 2.5 Pro大语言模型驱动。

Result: 系统在多个赛道中取得排名：问题解释第1名、答案生成第5名、证据识别第7名、答案-证据对齐第9名。

Conclusion: 将大型语言模型集成到结构化多阶段流水线中，能够提高接地性、精确性以及面向患者的健康沟通的专业质量。

Abstract: Patient portals now give individuals direct access to their electronic health records (EHRs), yet access alone does not ensure patients understand or act on the complex clinical information contained in these records. The ArchEHR-QA 2026 shared task addresses this challenge by focusing on grounded question answering over EHRs, and this paper presents the system developed by the HealthNLP_Retrievers team for this task. The proposed approach uses a multi-stage cascaded pipeline powered by the Gemini 2.5 Pro large language model to interpret patient-authored questions and retrieve relevant evidence from lengthy clinical notes. Our architecture comprises four integrated modules: (1) a few-shot query reformulation unit which summarizes verbose patient queries; (2) a heuristic-based evidence scorer which ranks clinical sentences to prioritize recall; (3) a grounded response generator which synthesizes professional-caliber answers restricted strictly to identified evidence; and (4) a high-precision many-to-many alignment framework which links generated answers to supporting clinical sentences. This cascaded approach achieved competitive results. Across the individual tracks, the system ranked 1st in question interpretation, 5th in answer generation, 7th in evidence identification, and 9th in answer-evidence alignment. These results show that integrating large language models within a structured multi-stage pipeline improves grounding, precision, and the professional quality of patient-oriented health communication. To support reproducibility, our source code is publicly available in our GitHub repository

</details>


### [122] [ClawGym: A Scalable Framework for Building Effective Claw Agents](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.26904)
*Fei Bai,Huatong Song,Shuang Sun,Daixuan Cheng,Yike Yang,Chuan Hao,Renyuan Li,Feng Chang,Yuan Wei,Ran Tao,Bryan Dai,Jian Yang,Wayne Xin Zhao*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: ClawGym是一个专为Claw风格个人智能体开发的全生命周期框架，包含13.5K合成任务数据集（ClawGym-SynData）、通过监督微调和强化学习训练的智能体模型（ClawGym-Agents），以及200个实例的评测基准（ClawGym-Bench），旨在解决此类环境中训练数据合成、智能体训练和诊断评估的系统性缺失问题。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: Claw风格环境支持多步骤工作流，但缺乏系统化框架来合成可验证的训练数据并将其与智能体训练和诊断评估相集成，这制约了该领域的可扩展发展。

Method: 构建ClawGym-SynData（13.5K个通过人物驱动意图和技能基础操作合成的过滤任务，搭配模拟工作空间和混合验证机制）；通过监督微调（基于黑盒rollout轨迹）和轻量级强化学习管道（并行化跨任务沙箱的rollout）训练ClawGym-Agents；构建ClawGym-Bench基准（200个实例，经自动过滤和人-LLM联合审核校准）。

Result: 生成了13.5K过滤任务数据集，训练了系列Claw风格模型，构建了200实例的评测基准。

Conclusion: ClawGym为Claw风格个人智能体开发提供了从数据合成、模型训练到评测的全生命周期系统化框架，解决了该领域关键的基础设施缺失问题。

Abstract: Claw-style environments support multi-step workflows over local files, tools, and persistent workspace states. However, scalable development around these environments remains constrained by the absence of a systematic framework, especially one for synthesizing verifiable training data and integrating it with agent training and diagnostic evaluation. To address this challenge, we present ClawGym, a scalable framework that supports the full lifecycle of Claw-style personal agent development. Concretely, we construct ClawGym-SynData, a diverse dataset of 13.5K filtered tasks synthesized from persona-driven intents and skill-grounded operations, paired with realistic mock workspaces and hybrid verification mechanisms. We then train a family of capable Claw-style models, termed ClawGym-Agents, through supervised fine-tuning on black-box rollout trajectories, and further explore reinforcement learning via a lightweight pipeline that parallelizes rollouts across per-task sandboxes.To support reliable evaluation, we further construct ClawGym-Bench, a benchmark of 200 instances calibrated through automated filtering and human-LLM review. Relevant resources will be soon released at https://github.com/ClawGym.

</details>


### [123] [Select to Think: Unlocking SLM Potential with Local Sufficiency](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.26940)
*Wenxuan Ye,Yangyang Zhang,Xueli An,Georg Carle,Yunpu Ma*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 针对小语言模型（SLM）推理能力不足的问题，本文提出SELECT TO THINK (S2T)方法，将大语言模型（LLM）的角色从开放生成转变为在SLM的Top-K候选中进行选择，并进一步提出S2T-LOCAL将这种选择逻辑蒸馏进SLM自身，使其无需LLM参与即可自主重排序。实验表明，1.5B的SLM其Top-8候选能覆盖32B LLM选择的95%，S2T-LOCAL将贪心解码平均提升24.1%，达到8路自一致性效果的同时保持单轨迹效率。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 小语言模型计算效率高但推理能力不足，现有方法在推理分歧点调用大语言模型生成token会引入大量延迟和成本，而标准蒸馏又受限于SLM容量难以准确模仿LLM的复杂生成分布。本文旨在解决这一两难困境。

Method: 提出SELECT TO THINK (S2T)框架：首先识别出SLM与LLM在推理中的分歧点，发现在这些点上LLM偏好的token始终落在SLM的Top-K预测中；然后将LLM的角色从开放生成简化为在SLM的Top-K候选中进行选择，将监督信号简化为离散候选排序；进一步提出S2T-LOCAL，通过蒸馏将选择逻辑嵌入SLM，使其能自主执行重排序，推理时不再依赖LLM。

Result: 1.5B SLM的Top-8候选能覆盖32B LLM选择的95%命中率；S2T-LOCAL在多个基准上将贪心解码平均提升24.1%，效果与8路自一致性方法相当，但仅需单轨迹推理效率。

Conclusion: 通过将LLM从生成者转变为选择者，并蒸馏选择逻辑到SLM中，可以高效、低成本地提升小语言模型的推理能力，有效弥合SLM与LLM之间的推理差距。

Abstract: Small language models (SLMs) offer computational efficiency for scalable deployment, yet they often fall short of the reasoning power exhibited by their larger counterparts (LLMs). To mitigate this gap, current approaches invoke an LLM to generate tokens at points of reasoning divergence, but these external calls introduce substantial latency and costs. Alternatively, standard distillation is often hindered by the capacity limitation, as SLMs struggle to accurately mimic the LLM's complex generative distribution. We address this dilemma by identifying local sufficiency: at divergence points, the LLM's preferred token consistently resides within the SLM's top-K next-token predictions, even when failing to emerge as the SLM top-1 choice. We therefore propose SELECT TO THINK (S2T), which reframes the LLM's role from open-ended generation to selection among the SLM's proposals, simplifying the supervision signal to discrete candidate rankings. Leveraging this, we introduce S2T-LOCAL, which distills the selection logic into the SLM, empowering it to perform autonomous re-ranking without inference-time LLM dependency. Empirically, we demonstrate that a 1.5B SLM's top-8 candidates capture the 32B LLM's choice with 95% hit rate. Translating this potential into performance, S2T-LOCAL improves greedy decoding by 24.1% on average across benchmarks, effectively matching the efficacy of 8-path self-consistency while operating with single-trajectory efficiency.

</details>


### [124] [Turning the TIDE: Cross-Architecture Distillation for Diffusion Large Language Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.26951)
*Gongbo Zhang,Wen Wang,Ye Tian,Li Yuan*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 提出TIDE框架，首个实现跨架构扩散大语言模型（dLLM）蒸馏的方法，将8B密集和16B MoE教师模型蒸馏至0.6B学生模型，在8个基准上平均提升1.53分。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有dLLM蒸馏方法仅限同架构知识迁移，无法处理教师与学生架构、注意力机制和分词器不同的跨架构场景，而现有SOTA dLLM需要数十亿参数才能达到竞争性能，亟需高效的小模型蒸馏方法。

Method: TIDE包含三个模块：①TIDAL联合调控训练进度和扩散时间步的蒸馏强度，适配教师依赖噪声的可靠性；②CompDemo通过互补掩码分割丰富教师上下文，改善高掩码比例下的预测；③Reverse CALM通过反转块级似然匹配实现跨分词器目标，提供有界梯度和双端噪声过滤。

Result: 将8B密集教师和16B MoE教师通过两条异构流水线蒸馏至0.6B学生模型，在8个基准上平均超越基线1.53分，代码生成任务上HumanEval得分48.78（AR基线32.3）。

Conclusion: TIDE是首个有效的跨架构dLLM蒸馏框架，显著提升了小模型在多项任务上的性能，尤其在代码生成领域表现突出。

Abstract: Diffusion large language models (dLLMs) offer parallel decoding and bidirectional context, but state-of-the-art dLLMs require billions of parameters for competitive performance. While existing distillation methods for dLLMs reduce inference steps within a single architecture, none address cross-architecture knowledge transfer, in which the teacher and student differ in architecture, attention mechanism, and tokenizer. We present TIDE, the first framework for cross-architecture dLLM distillation, comprising three modular components: (1) TIDAL, which jointly modulates distillation strength across training progress and diffusion timestep to account for the teacher's noise-dependent reliability; (2) CompDemo, which enriches the teacher's context via complementary mask splitting to improve predictions under heavy masking; and (3) Reverse CALM, a cross-tokenizer objective that inverts chunk-level likelihood matching, yielding bounded gradients and dual-end noise filtering. Distilling 8B dense and 16B MoE teachers into a 0.6B student via two heterogeneous pipelines outperforms the baseline by an average of 1.53 points across eight benchmarks, yielding notable gains in code generation, where HumanEval scores reach 48.78 compared to 32.3 for the AR baseline.

</details>


<div id='cs.AI'></div>

# cs.AI [[Back]](#toc)

### [125] [Distill-Belief: Closed-Loop Inverse Source Localization and Characterization in Physical Fields](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.26095)
*Yiwei Shi,Zixing Song,Mengyue Yang,Cunjia Liu,Weiru Liu*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 提出Distill-Belief师生框架，用贝叶斯教师提供信息增益信号，让学生蒸馏后验信念用于控制和停止决策，在减少感知成本的同时避免奖励黑客问题，实现高效可靠的逆源定位与表征。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 闭环逆源定位与表征需要在严格时间约束下，由移动智能体选择测量来定位源并推断潜在场参数。核心挑战在于信念空间目标函数：准确的贝叶斯推理计算昂贵，而快速学习的信念模型会导致奖励黑客问题（策略利用近似误差而非真正降低不确定性）。

Method: 提出Distill-Belief师生框架：教师使用贝叶斯正确的粒子滤波器维护后验并提供密集信息增益信号；学生将后验蒸馏为用于控制的信念统计量和用于停止的不确定性证书。部署时仅使用学生模型，每步计算成本恒定。

Result: 在七种场模态和两种压力测试上，Distill-Belief相比基线方法一致地降低了感知成本，并提高了成功率、后验收缩和估计精度，同时缓解了奖励黑客问题。

Conclusion: 通过解耦正确性与效率，Distill-Belief框架在保证贝叶斯正确性的同时实现了高效部署，有效解决了奖励黑客问题，在多个场模态下均优于现有方法。

Abstract: {Closed-loop inverse source localization and characterization (ISLC) requires a mobile agent to select measurements that localize sources and infer latent field parameters under strict time constraints.} {The core challenge lies in the belief-space objective: valid uncertainty estimation requires expensive Bayesian inference, whereas using fast learned belief model leads to reward hacking, in which the policy exploits approximation errors rather than actually reducing uncertainty.} {We propose \textbf{Distill-Belief}, a teacher--student framework that decouples correctness from efficiency. A Bayes-correct particle-filter teacher maintains the posterior and supplies a dense information-gain signal, while a compact student distills the posterior into belief statistics for control and an uncertainty certificate for stopping. At deployment, only the student is used, yielding constant per-step cost.} {Experiments on seven field modalities and two stress tests show that Distill-Belief consistently reduces sensing cost and improves success, posterior contraction, and estimation accuracy over baselines, while mitigating reward hacking.}

</details>


### [126] [Hierarchical Multi-Persona Induction from User Behavioral Logs: Learning Evidence-Grounded and Truthful Personas](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.26120)
*Nayoung Choi,Haeyu Jeong,Changbong Kim,Hongjun Lim,Jinho D. Choi*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 提出分层框架，将用户行为日志聚合为意图记忆，通过聚类和标注生成多视角、有证据支撑的用户画像，并使用分组DPO优化画像的连贯性、证据对齐和真实性，实验表明该方法生成更高质量画像并提升下游预测性能。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 行为日志信号丰富但嘈杂且意图交错，现有LLM生成可解释画像的方法主要关注下游效用，缺乏对画像本身质量的评估保障。

Method: 构建分层框架：将用户行为聚合为意图记忆，通过聚类和标注生成多证据画像；将画像归纳形式化为优化问题，目标包括聚类凝聚性、画像-证据对齐度和画像真实性；使用分组扩展的Direct Preference Optimization（DPO）训练画像模型。

Result: 在大规模服务日志和两个公开数据集上，该方法生成的画像更连贯、更有证据支撑、更可信，同时在未来交互预测任务上也取得更优表现。

Conclusion: 所提分层框架和分组DPO优化方法能有效提升用户画像的自身质量（连贯性、证据对齐、真实性），且高质量画像有助于提升下游任务性能。

Abstract: Behavioral logs provide rich signals for user modeling, but are noisy and interleaved across diverse intents. Recent work uses LLMs to generate interpretable natural-language personas from user logs, yet evaluation often emphasizes downstream utility, providing limited assurance of persona quality itself. We propose a hierarchical framework that aggregates user actions into intent memories and induces multiple evidence-grounded personas by clustering and labeling these memories. We formulate persona induction as an optimization problem over persona quality-captured by cluster cohesion, persona-evidence alignment, and persona truthfulness-and train the persona model using a groupwise extension of Direct Preference Optimization (DPO). Experiments on a large-scale service log and two public datasets show that our method induces more coherent, evidence-grounded, and trustworthy personas, while also improving future interaction prediction.

</details>


### [127] [OMEGA: Optimizing Machine Learning by Evaluating Generated Algorithms](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.26211)
*Jeremy Nixon,Annika Singh*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: OMEGA是一个全流程自动化AI研究框架，从想法生成到可执行代码，通过结构化元提示工程与代码生成结合，创建了多个超越scikit-learn基线的新ML分类算法，在20个基准数据集上验证有效。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 实现AI研究的全自动化，从想法生成到可执行代码的端到端流程，减少人工干预。

Method: 结合结构化元提示工程与可执行代码生成，创建新的机器学习分类器。

Result: 生成的多个新算法在20个基准数据集（infinity-bench）上超越scikit-learn基线。

Conclusion: OMEGA框架能够自动化生成性能优于传统基线的新ML算法，并已封装为Python包供使用。

Abstract: In order to automate AI research we introduce a full, end-to-end framework, OMEGA: Optimizing Machine learning by Evaluating Generated Algorithms, that starts at idea generation and ends with executable code. Our system combines structured meta-prompt engineering with executable code generation to create new ML classifiers. The OMEGA framework has been utilized to generate several novel algorithms that outperform scikit-learn baselines across a robust selection of 20 benchmark datasets (infinity-bench). You can access models discussed in this paper and more in the python package: pip install omega-models.

</details>


### [128] [Persuadability and LLMs as Legal Decision Tools](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.26233)
*Oisin Suttle,David Lillis*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文探究了前沿开源和闭源大语言模型（LLMs）在法律决策中对法律论证的响应方式，特别是论证质量如何影响模型采纳特定法律观点的可能性，并分析了驱动因素。实验结果对LLMs在法律和行政场景中的可行性具有重要意义。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 随着LLMs被提议作为法律决策助手甚至一审决策者，理解它们如何回答法律问题，尤其是影响它们在疑难问题上做出判断的因素，变得至关重要。法律决策的一个特定特征是必须回应争议双方的论点，决策者既要能够被论点说服，又不能被辩护技巧过度影响而忽视案件实质。

Method: 通过原始实验，考察前沿开源和闭源LLMs对法律论证的响应，系统性地检验辩护方提出的论证质量如何影响模型同意特定法律观点的可能性，并探索驱动这些结果的因素。

Result: 报告了前沿开源和闭源LLMs对法律论证响应的实验性结果，揭示了论证质量对模型法律观点采纳概率的影响模式及其背后的驱动因素。

Conclusion: 研究结果对LLMs在法律和行政场景中的部署可行性具有重要启示，指出了模型在法律论证响应方面存在的风险与挑战。

Abstract: As Large Language Models (LLMs) are proposed as legal decision assistants, and even first-instance decision-makers, across a range of judicial and administrative contexts, it becomes essential to explore how they answer legal questions, and in particular the factors that lead them to decide difficult questions in one way or another. A specific feature of legal decisions is the need to respond to arguments advanced by contending parties. A legal decision-maker must be able to engage with, and respond to, including through being potentially persuaded by, arguments advanced by the parties. Conversely, they should not be unduly persuadable, influenced by a particularly compelling advocate to decide cases based on the skills of the advocates, rather than the merits of the case. We explore how frontier open- and closed-weights LLMs respond to legal arguments, reporting original experimental results examining how the quality of the advocate making those arguments affects the likelihood that a model will agree with a particular legal point of view, and exploring the factors driving these results. Our results have implications for the feasibility of adopting LLMs across legal and administrative settings.

</details>


### [129] [Apriori-based Analysis of Learned Helplessness in Mathematics Tutoring: Behavioral Patterns by Level, Intervention, and Outcome](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.26237)
*John Paul P. Miranda*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本研究使用Apriori算法分析数学辅导系统日志中与习得性无助相关的行为交互模式，发现低LH学生更倾向于坚持和成功，高LH学生更多表现出回避模式，不同干预条件下行为模式存在差异。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 探究数学辅导系统中学生习得性无助的行为交互模式，理解不同LH水平、系统干预条件和问题解决结果下的行为差异，为教育干预提供数据驱动的依据。

Method: 采用Apriori关联规则挖掘算法，从LH水平（低/高）、系统干预（有/无）和问题解决结果（解决/未解决）三个维度分析数学辅导系统日志中的行为交互模式。

Result: 1）跳过问题且不使用提示是未解决结果最频繁的模式；2）低LH学生坚持行为与成功关联更强，提示使用与解决结果正相关；3）高LH学生更多回避模式，跳过与未解决结果强相关；4）无干预组坚持-成功关联最强，有干预组跳过-未解决模式更强；5）不跳过一致关联成功，无提示跳过预测未解决。

Conclusion: 不同LH水平和干预条件下学生行为模式存在显著差异，研究结果为针对性教育干预提供实践建议。

Abstract: This study applied the Apriori algorithm to analyze behavioral interaction patterns associated with learned helplessness (LH) in mathematics tutoring system logs. Interaction data were examined across three dimensions: LH level (low vs. high), system-based intervention (with vs. without), and problem-solving outcomes (solved vs. unsolved). The analysis of the complete dataset showed that skipping problems without using hints was the most frequent pattern linked to unsolved outcomes, while persistence behaviors such as not skipping were less dominant overall. Comparisons by LH level showed that low-LH students had stronger links between problem solving and not skipping, as well as positive associations between hint use and solved outcomes. High-LH students showed more avoidance patterns, with skipping strongly tied to unsolved outcomes. In the comparison of system-based intervention conditions, students without intervention had the highest lift for persistence-success links, while the with-intervention group had stronger patterns involving skipping behaviors leading to unsolved outcomes. Outcome-specific analysis showed that not skipping was consistently associated with solved problems across all groups, while skipping without hints predicted unsolved outcomes. Practical implications and recommendations are discussed.

</details>


### [130] [DreamProver: Evolving Transferable Lemma Libraries via a Wake-Sleep Theorem-Proving Agent](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.26311)
*Youyuan Zhang,Jialiang Sun,Hangrui Bi,Chuqin Geng,Wenjie Ma,Zhaoyu Li,Xujie Si*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: DreamProver是一个基于"唤醒-睡眠"程序归纳范式的智能体框架，通过交替进行定理证明尝试与引理提炼压缩，自动发现可复用的高层次引理，显著提升形式化定理证明的成功率、简洁性和计算效率。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有方法要么依赖固定引理库导致适应性差，要么为单个定理合成高度特化的中间引理而缺乏通用性，亟需一种能自动发现可迁移、可复用引理的方法。

Method: 采用"唤醒-睡眠"两阶段迭代过程：唤醒阶段用当前引理库证明训练集定理并提出候选新引理；睡眠阶段对候选引理进行抽象、精炼和整合，压缩优化引理库，逐步演化出一组紧凑、高层、可迁移的引理。

Result: 在多个数学基准测试上显著提升了证明成功率，同时生成了更简洁的证明并降低了计算成本。

Conclusion: DreamProver通过迭代式引理发现与压缩机制，成功实现了引理库的自适应演化，在提高证明能力的同时兼顾了效率和简洁性。

Abstract: We introduce DreamProver, an agentic framework that leverages a "wake-sleep" program induction paradigm to discover reusable lemmas for formal theorem proving. Existing approaches either rely on fixed lemma libraries, which limit adaptability, or synthesize highly specific intermediate lemmas tailored to individual theorems, thereby lacking generality. DreamProver addresses this gap through an iterative two-stage process. In the wake stage, DreamProver attempts to prove theorems from a training set using the current lemma library while proposing new candidate lemmas. In the "sleep" stage, it abstracts, refines, and consolidates these candidates to compress and optimize the library. Through this alternating cycle, DreamProver progressively evolves a compact set of high-level, transferable lemmas that can be effectively used to prove unseen theorems in related domains. Experimental results demonstrate that DreamProver substantially improves proof success rates across a diverse set of mathematical benchmarks, while also producing more concise proofs and reducing computational cost.

</details>


### [131] [AGEL-Comp: A Neuro-Symbolic Framework for Compositional Generalization in Interactive Agents](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.26522)
*Mahnoor Shahid,Hannes Rothe*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 提出AGEL-Comp神经符号智能体架构，通过动态因果程序图、归纳逻辑编程引擎和混合推理核心，解决LLM智能体在组合泛化中的系统性失败问题。在Retro Quest仿真环境中的评估表明，该模型优于纯LLM模型。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 基于LLM的智能体在组合泛化方面存在系统性失败，限制了其在交互环境中的鲁棒性。

Method: 提出AGEL-Comp架构，包含三项创新：(1) 动态因果程序图(CPG)作为世界模型；(2) 归纳逻辑编程(ILP)引擎从经验反馈中合成新Horn子句；(3) 混合推理核心，LLM提出候选子目标，神经定理证明器(NTP)验证逻辑一致性。实现演绎-溯因学习循环。

Result: 在Retro Quest仿真环境中的评估表明，AGEL模型优于纯LLM模型。

Conclusion: 该框架为构建具有显式、可解释且组合结构化世界理解的智能体提供了原则性路径。

Abstract: Large Language Model (LLM)-based agents exhibit systemic failures in compositional generalization, limiting their robustness in interactive environments. This work introduces AGEL-Comp, a neuro-symbolic AI agent architecture designed to address this challenge by grounding actions of the agent. AGEL-Comp integrates three core innovations: (1) a dynamic Causal Program Graph (CPG) as a world model, representing procedural and causal knowledge as a directed hypergraph; (2) an Inductive Logic Programming (ILP) engine that synthesizes new Horn clauses from experiential feedback, grounding symbolic knowledge through interaction; and (3) a hybrid reasoning core where an LLM proposes a set of candidate sub-goals that are verified for logical consistency by a Neural Theorem Prover (NTP). Together, these components operationalize a deduction--abduction learning cycle: enabling the agent to deduce plans and abductively expand its symbolic world model, while a neural adaptation phase keeps its reasoning engine aligned with new knowledge. We propose an evaluation protocol within the \texttt{Retro Quest} simulation environment to probe for compositional generalization scenarios to evaluate our AGEL agent. Our findings clearly indicate the better performance of our AGEL model over pure LLM-based models. Our framework presents a principled path toward agents that build an explicit, interpretable, and compositionally structured understanding of their world.

</details>


### [132] [Benchmarking the Safety of Large Language Models for Robotic Health Attendant Control](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.26577)
*Mahiro Nakao,Kazuhiro Takemoto*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 该研究构建了基于AMA医学伦理的270条有害指令数据集，在机器人健康助手模拟环境中评估了72个LLM，发现平均违规率54.4%，开放权重模型安全性能远差于闭源模型（中位72.8% vs 23.7%），医学领域微调和提示防御策略效果有限，强调安全评估应作为LLM部署的首要标准。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: LLM作为机器人健康助手的控制组件正被考虑部署，但其在此场景下的安全性尚未得到充分表征。

Method: 基于AMA医学伦理原则构建9类270条有害指令数据集，在机器人健康助手仿真环境中评估72个LLM的违规率。

Result: 平均违规率54.4%，超半数模型超50%；设备操纵和紧急延迟等表面合理指令更难拒绝；开放权重模型（中位72.8%）远差于闭源模型（23.7%）；模型规模和发布日期是安全性的主要决定因素；医学领域微调和提示防御策略效果有限。

Conclusion: 安全评估必须作为LLM开发和部署的首要标准。

Abstract: Large language models (LLMs) are increasingly considered for deployment as the control component of robotic health attendants, yet their safety in this context remains poorly characterized. We introduce a dataset of 270 harmful instructions spanning nine prohibited behavior categories grounded in the American Medical Association Principles of Medical Ethics, and use it to evaluate 72 LLMs in a simulation environment based on the Robotic Health Attendant framework. The mean violation rate across all models was 54.4\%, with more than half exceeding 50\%, and violation rates varied substantially across behavior categories, with superficially plausible instructions such as device manipulation and emergency delay proving harder to refuse than overtly destructive ones. Model size and release date were the primary determinants of safety performance among open-weight models, and proprietary models were substantially safer than open-weight counterparts (median 23.7\% versus 72.8\%). Medical domain fine-tuning conferred no significant overall safety benefit, and a prompt-based defense strategy produced only a modest reduction in violation rates among the least safe models, leaving absolute violation rates at levels that would preclude safe clinical deployment. These findings demonstrate that safety evaluation must be treated as a first-class criterion in the development and deployment of LLMs for robotic health attendants.

</details>


### [133] [Human-in-the-Loop Benchmarking of Heterogeneous LLMs for Automated Competency Assessment in Secondary Level Mathematics](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.26607)
*Jatin Bhusal,Nancy Mahatha,Aayush Acharya,Raunak Regmi*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文提出一个“人在回路中”的基准测试框架，用于评估多个LLM在自动化中学数学评估中的有效性。基于尼泊尔10年级选修数学课程，创建了包含四个主题和四项跨领域能力的多维评分表。对四个模型（Eagle、Orion、Nova、Lyra）进行基准测试，发现存在明显的“架构兼容性差距”：基于Gemini的稀疏MoE模型达到“公平一致性”（kappa_w~0.38），而更大的Orion（70B）模型却呈现“无一致性”（kappa_w=-0.0261），表明在基于评分表的任务中，架构对指令约束的遵循比原始参数规模更重要。结论是LLM尚不适合自主认证，但可在“人在回路中”框架内为初步证据提取提供高价值的辅助支持。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 基于能力的教育（CBE）在全球兴起，但从基于分数的评估转向定性能力映射对教育者来说是手动挑战。本文旨在解决这一瓶颈问题，通过评估多个LLM在自动化中学数学评估中的有效性来减轻教师负担。

Method: 提出了一个“人在回路中”的基准测试框架，基于尼泊尔10年级选修数学课程创建了包含四个主题和四项跨领域能力（理解、知识、操作流畅性、行为与相关性）的多维评分表。使用多供应商集成（包括开放权重模型Eagle和Orion，以及专有前沿模型Nova和Lyra），以两位高级数学教师确定的真值作为基准（kappa_w=0.8652）进行对比评估。

Result: 存在明显的“架构兼容性差距”：基于Gemini的稀疏MoE模型达到“公平一致性”（kappa_w~0.38），而更大的Orion（70B）模型呈现“无一致性”（kappa_w=-0.0261）。这表明架构对指令约束的遵循比原始参数规模更重要。

Conclusion: LLM尚不适合自主认证，但可在“人在回路中”框架内为初步证据提取提供高价值的辅助支持。

Abstract: As Competency-Based Education (CBE) is gaining traction around the world, the shift from marks-based assessment to qualitative competency mapping is a manual challenge for educators. This paper tackles the bottleneck issue by suggesting a "Human-in-the-Loop" benchmarking framework to assess the effectiveness of multiple LLMs in automating secondary-level mathematics assessment. Based on the Grade 10 Optional Mathematics curriculum in Nepal, we created a multi-dimensional rubric for four topics and four cross-cutting competencies: Comprehension, Knowledge, Operational Fluency, and Behavior and Correlation.
  The multi-provider ensemble, consisted of open-weight models -- Eagle (Llama 3.1-8B) and Orion (Llama 3.3-70B) -- and proprietary frontier models Nova (Gemini 2.5 Flash) and Lyra (Gemini 3 Pro), was benchmarked against a ground truth defined by two senior mathematics faculty members (kappa_w = 0.8652). The findings show a marked "Architecture-compatibility gap". Although the Gemini-based Mixture-of-Experts (Sparse MoE) models achieved "Fair Agreement" (kappa_w ~ 0.38), the larger Orion (70B) model exhibited "No Agreement" (kappa_w = -0.0261), suggesting that architectural compliance with instruction constraints outweighs the scale of raw parameters in rubric-constrained tasks. We conclude that while LLMs are not yet suitable for autonomous certification, they provide high-value assistive support for preliminary evidence extraction within a "Human-in-the-Loop" framework.

</details>


### [134] [When to Vote, When to Rewrite: Disagreement-Guided Strategy Routing for Test-Time Scaling](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.26644)
*Zhimin Lin,Yixin Ji,Jinpeng Li,Yu Luo,Dong Li,Junhua Fang,Juntao Li,Min Zhang*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 提出了一种基于输出分歧的训练无关框架，将测试时扩展建模为实例级路由问题，根据输出不一致程度动态选择不同策略，在7个数学基准上提升3%-7%准确率并降低采样成本。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有测试时扩展方法（如重复采样、自我修正、树搜索）在困难问题上收益递减，且缺乏针对实例难度的自适应策略选择机制。

Method: 利用输出分歧作为实例难度和预测正确性的信号，构建训练无关的实例级路由框架：一致性高的实例用轻量级解析，中度分歧用多数投票，高度模糊用重写式重表述。

Result: 在7个数学基准和3个模型上，相比现有方法提升3%-7%准确率，同时降低采样成本。

Conclusion: 输出分歧可作为测试时策略选择的有效信号，基于路由的实例级方法比单一策略分配更多计算更高效。

Abstract: Large Reasoning Models (LRMs) achieve strong performance on mathematical reasoning tasks but remain unreliable on challenging instances. Existing test-time scaling methods, such as repeated sampling, self-correction, and tree search, improve performance at the cost of increased computation, yet often exhibit diminishing returns on hard problems. We observe that output disagreement is strongly correlated with instance difficulty and prediction correctness, providing a useful signal for guiding instance-level strategy selection at test time. Based on this insight, we propose a training-free framework that formulates test-time scaling as an instance-level routing problem, rather than allocating more computation within a single strategy, dynamically selecting among different scaling strategies based on output disagreement. The framework applies lightweight resolution for consistent cases, majority voting for moderate disagreement, and rewriting-based reformulation for highly ambiguous instances. Experiments on seven mathematical benchmarks and three models show that our method improves accuracy by 3% - 7% while reducing sampling cost compared to existing approaches.

</details>


### [135] [SciHorizon-DataEVA: An Agentic System for AI-Readiness Evaluation of Heterogeneous Scientific Data](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.26645)
*Dianyu Liu,Chuan Qin,Xi Chen,Xiaohan Li,Wenxi Xu,Yuyang Wang,Xin Chen,Yuanchun Zhou,Hengshu Zhu*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文提出SciHorizon-DataEVA，一个用于可扩展评估异构科学数据AI就绪性的智能体系统，通过Sci-TQA2原则（涵盖治理可信度、数据质量、AI兼容性和科学适应性四个维度）和分层多智能体评估方法Sci-TQA2-Eval，实现了细粒度、可执行的数据AI就绪性评估。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 科学数据缺乏可扩展且系统的AI就绪性评估机制，制约了AI4Science模型的有效性。

Method: 提出Sci-TQA2原则将AI就绪性分解为四个互补维度及可度量原子元素；开发Sci-TQA2-Eval分层多智能体评估方法，通过有向循环工作流（包含数据集画像、适用性感知指标激活、知识增强规划及自适应工具中心化评估机制）实现可扩展评估。

Result: 在跨多个领域的科学数据集上进行的广泛实验验证了SciHorizon-DataEVA在原则化AI就绪性评估方面的有效性和通用性。

Conclusion: SciHorizon-DataEVA为科学数据的AI就绪性评估提供了首个系统化、可扩展的解决方案，填补了该领域的方法空白。

Abstract: AI-for-Science (AI4Science) is increasingly transforming scientific discovery by embedding machine learning models into prediction, simulation, and hypothesis generation workflows across domains. However, the effectiveness of these models is fundamentally constrained by the AI-readiness of scientific data, for which no scalable and systematic evaluation mechanism currently exists. In this work, we propose SciHorizon-DataEVA, a novel agentic system to scalable AI-readiness evaluation of heterogeneous scientific data. At the evaluation-criteria level, we introduce the Sci-TQA2 principles, which organize AI-readiness into four complementary dimensions: Governance Trustworthiness, Data Quality, AI Compatibility, and Scientific Adaptability. Each dimension is decomposed into measurable atomic elements that enable fine-grained and executable assessment. To operationalize these principles at scale, we develop Sci-TQA2-Eval, a hierarchical multi-agent evaluation approach orchestrated through a directed, cyclic workflow. Our Sci-TQA2-Eval dynamically constructs dataset-aware evaluation specifications by combining lightweight dataset profiling, applicability-aware metric activation, and knowledge-augmented planning grounded in domain constraints and dataset-paper signals. These specifications are executed through an adaptive, tool-centric evaluation mechanism with built-in verification and self-correction, enabling scalable and reliable assessment across heterogeneous scientific data. Extensive experiments on scientific datasets spanning multiple domains demonstrate the effectiveness and generality of SciHorizon-DataEVA for principled AI-readiness evaluation.

</details>


### [136] [FutureWorld: A Live Environment for Training Predictive Agents with Real-World Outcome Rewards](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.26733)
*Zhixin Han,Yanzhi Zhang,Chuyang Wei,Maohang Gao,Xiawei Yue,Kefei Chen,Yu Zhuang,Haoxiang Guan,Jiyan He,Jian Li,Yitong Duan,Yu Shi,Mengting Hu,Shuxin Zheng*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文提出FutureWorld，一个基于实时未来预测的智能体强化学习环境，通过"预测-结果-参数更新"闭环训练机制，利用真实世界事件进行持续学习，实验证明训练有效并建立了基准评测。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有实时未来预测研究分散于不同方向，缺乏统一的学习环境框架；同时，未来预测任务能提供大量基于真实世界事件的预测问题且避免答案泄漏，是智能体持续学习的理想场景。

Method: 构建FutureWorld智能体强化学习环境，实现"预测→结果实现→参数更新"的训练闭环；选取三个开源基座模型进行连续多日训练；基于该环境构建每日评测基准。

Result: 连续多日训练结果表明训练过程有效；在建立的每日基准上评估了多个前沿智能体系统，建立了当前智能体系统的性能基线。

Conclusion: FutureWorld作为统一的实时未来预测学习环境是有效的，能够驱动智能体通过真实世界事件进行持续强化学习，为未来智能体系统研究提供了有价值的基准平台。

Abstract: Live future prediction refers to the task of making predictions about real-world events before they unfold. This task is increasingly studied using large language model-based agent systems, and it is important for building agents that can continually learn from real-world. Just as interactive environments have often driven progress in agents, advancing live future prediction naturally motivates viewing it as a learning environment. Prior works have explored future prediction from several different parts, but have generally not framed it as a unified learning environment. This task is appealing for learning because it can provide a large number of prediction questions grounded in diverse real-world events, while preventing answer leakage. To leverage the advantages of live future prediction, we present FutureWorld, a live agentic reinforcement learning environment that closes the training loop between prediction, outcome realization, and parameters update. In our environment, we take three open-source base models and train them for consecutive days. The results show that training is effective. Furthermore, we build a daily benchmark based on the environment and evaluate several frontier agents on it to establish performance baselines for current agent systems.

</details>


### [137] [Bian Que: An Agentic Framework with Flexible Skill Arrangement for Online System Operations](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.26805)
*Bochao Liu,Zhipeng Qian,Yang Zhao,Xinyuan Jiang,Zihan Liang,Yufei Ma,Junpeng Zhuang,Ben Chen,Shuo Yang,Hongen Wan,Yao Wu,Chenyi Lei,Xiao Liang*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文提出Bian Que，一个面向大规模在线引擎系统运维的智能体框架，通过统一运维范式、灵活技能编排和自进化机制，在快手电商搜索场景中减少75%告警、实现80%根因分析准确率并降低50%以上平均修复时间。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 大规模在线引擎系统（搜索、推荐、广告）的运维高度依赖人工，而基于LLM的智能体面临的核心瓶颈并非推理能力，而是编排问题——如何为每个运维事件精准匹配相关数据和知识。手动维护映射关系在每日数十次发布的节奏下不可行，全量输入又会导致信息稀释和幻觉。

Method: 提出三个贡献：(i) 统一运维范式，将日常运维抽象为三种规范模式：发布拦截、主动排查、告警根因分析；(ii) 灵活技能编排（Flexible Skill Arrangement），每个Skill定义针对特定业务模块上下文的检索策略，可由LLM自动生成更新或通过自然语言指令迭代优化；(iii) 统一自进化机制，一条纠错信号同时驱动案例记忆到知识的蒸馏和针对性的Skill优化两条路径。

Result: 在快手电商搜索引擎部署后，告警量减少75%，根因分析准确率达80%，平均修复时间降低50%以上；离线评估通过率达99.0%。

Conclusion: Bian Que通过将运维知识以Skill形式结构化编排，配合自进化机制，有效解决了LLM运维智能体的编排瓶颈，显著提升了大规模在线系统的运维效率。

Abstract: Operating and maintaining (O&M) large-scale online engine systems (search, recommendation, advertising) demands substantial human effort for release monitoring, alert response, and root cause analysis. While LLM-based agents are a natural fit for these tasks, the deployment bottleneck is not reasoning capability but orchestration: selecting, for each operational event, the relevant data (metrics, logs, change events) and the applicable operational knowledge (handbook rules and practitioner experience). Feeding all signals indiscriminately causes dilution and hallucination, while manually curating the event-to-(data, knowledge) mapping is intractable under dozens of daily releases. We present Bian Que, an agentic framework with three contributions: (i) a \emph{unified operational paradigm} abstracting day-to-day O&M into three canonical patterns: release interception, proactive inspection, and alert root cause analysis; (ii) \emph{Flexible Skill Arrangement}, where each Skill specifies which data and knowledge to retrieve for a given business-module context and can be automatically generated and updated by LLMs or iteratively refined through natural-language instructions from on-call engineers; (iii) a \emph{unified self-evolving mechanism} in which one correction signal drives two parallel pathways, case-memory-to-knowledge distillation and targeted Skill refinement. Deployed on the e-commerce search engine of KuaiShou, the major short-video platform in China, Bian Que reduces alert volume by 75%, achieves 80% root-cause analysis accuracy, and cuts mean time to resolution by over 50%. Our framework achieves 99.0% pass rate on offline evaluations. Our code is available at https://github.com/benchen4395/BianQue_Assistant.

</details>


<div id='cs.RO'></div>

# cs.RO [[Back]](#toc)

### [138] [FalconApp: Rapid iPhone Deployment of End-to-End Perception via Automatically Labeled Synthetic Data](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.25949)
*Yan Miao,Will Shen,Sayan Mitra*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: FalconApp是一个将iPhone手持拍摄视频转化为目标检测与6D姿态估计感知模块的端到端iOS应用，通过高斯泼溅重建、照片级自动标注和移动端部署流水线，平均20分钟即可为单个物体生成可用模型，在iPhone上实现约30ms端到端延迟。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 机器人感知依赖大规模标注数据，但真实数据集的人工标注耗时且成本高昂，亟需一种快速自动化生成感知模型的方法。

Method: 用户通过iPhone拍摄物体视频；FalconApp重建可编辑的GSplat资产；将该资产与多样化照片级背景合成；渲染带有真值掩码和位姿的合成图像；训练感知模块；最后部署回iPhone前端。

Result: 在5个不同几何外观的刚性物体上，平均约20分钟即可完成合成数据生成与训练；iPhone端到端延迟约30ms；在仿真和真实评估中，4/5物体的姿态精度优于PnP基线方法。

Conclusion: FalconApp展示了利用移动端设备快速生成机器人感知模块的可行性，大幅降低了数据标注成本和时间开销。

Abstract: Reliable perception for robotics depends on large-scale labeled data, yet real-world datasets rely on heavy manual annotation and are time-consuming to produce. We present FalconApp, an iPhone app with an end-to-end frontend-backend pipeline that turns a short handheld capture of a rigid object into a perception module for mask detection and 6-DoF pose estimation. Our core contribution is a rapid mobile deployment pipeline paired with a photorealistic auto-labeling workflow: from a user-captured video of an object, FalconApp reconstructs an editable GSplat asset, composites it with diverse photorealistic backgrounds, renders synthetic images with ground-truth masks and poses, trains the perception module, and deploys it back to the iPhone frontend. Experiments across five rigid objects with diverse geometry and appearance show that FalconApp produces usable perception models with about 20 minutes of synthetic-data generation and training per object on average, around 30 ms end-to-end on-device latency on iPhone, and better overall pose accuracy than a PnP baseline on 4 / 5 objects in both simulation and real-world evaluation.

</details>


### [139] [A Scaled Three-Vehicle Platooning Platform](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.25963)
*Kaiyue Lu,Qiaoxuan Zhang,Yukun Lu*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 本文介绍了用于自主队列研究的缩尺多车平台，包含一辆人工驾驶领航车和两辆自主跟随车，重点解决队列在动态 maneuvers（如变道）中的路径跟踪稳定性和鲁棒控制问题。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 队列行驶中领航车辆的横向偏差和航向扰动会向下游传播，影响跟随车辆的路径跟踪稳定性，亟需鲁棒的纵横向控制系统来保证个体跟踪性能和整体队列稳定性。

Method: IMRL实验室开发了一个缩尺多车平台，包含一辆可人工操控的领航车和两辆自主跟随车，用于队列控制和人机协同自主性的可控可重复实验。

Result: 该缩尺平台相比全尺寸实地测试更安全、成本更低、更灵活，适合快速原型开发、控制器验证和多智能体自主性研究，同时比纯仿真评估具有更强的物理真实性。

Conclusion: 缩尺多车平台是自主队列研究的有效实验工具，在安全性和物理真实性之间取得了良好平衡，尤其适用于协同控制和人机协同自主性的验证与评估。

Abstract: Vehicle platooning has attracted increasing attention as a promising approach to improve traffic efficiency, energy consumption, and roadway safety through coordinated multi-vehicle operation. A key challenge in platooning lies in maintaining stable and accurate path tracking during dynamic maneuvers such as lane changes, where lateral deviations and heading disturbances generated by the lead vehicle may propagate downstream to following vehicles. Robust longitudinal and lateral control systems are therefore essential not only for individual vehicle tracking performance, but also for overall platoon stability. For experimental studies, the Intelligent Mobility and Robotics Lab (IMRL) develops a scaled multi-vehicle platform for autonomous platooning research, with a particular emphasis on cooperative control and human-in-the-loop autonomy. This platform consists of one human-operable lead vehicle and two autonomous followers, enabling controlled and repeatable experiments on leader-follower coordination. Compared with full-scale field testing, this scaled platform offers a safer, lower-cost, and more flexible environment for rapid prototyping, controller validation, and multi-agent autonomy studies, while providing stronger physical realism than purely simulation-based evaluations.

</details>


### [140] [Multi-Periodogram Velocity Estimation with Irregular Reference Signals for Robot-Aided ISAC](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.25974)
*Yi Geng,Pan Cao,Ting Zeng,Yongqian Deng*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 本文研究了机器人辅助集成感知与通信（ISAC）中的速度估计问题，提出了一种基于多周期图的速度估计算法，该算法利用不规则5G/6G参考信号的时间域结构，无需修改3GPP标准即可提升低SNR下的鲁棒性，相比传统方法实现了3 dB SNR增益和51%的虚警率降低。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有ISAC系统中，移动机器人作为感知节点只能机会性地复用不规则的5G/6G参考信号，传统速度估计方法在低信噪比条件下性能不佳，需要一种无需新增专用感知参考信号或修改3GPP标准即可提高速度估计鲁棒性的方法。

Method: 首先将不规则时域模式诱导的速度剖面分解为周期性峰值分量和幅度整形（加权）分量；基于此结构，提出一种多周期图速度估计算法，该算法符合5G/6G标准，无需新的感知专用参考信号或3GPP修改。

Result: 仿真结果表明，与传统周期图处理方法相比，所提方法在10%漏检率下实现了3 dB的SNR增益，并将虚警率降低了51%。

Conclusion: 所提出的多周期图速度估计算法在保持标准兼容性的同时，显著提升了低SNR条件下的速度估计鲁棒性，为机器人辅助ISAC系统提供了一种有效的速度估计方案。

Abstract: This paper addresses velocity estimation within robot-aided integrated sensing and communications (ISAC), where mobile robots act as sensing nodes but can only opportunistically reuse irregular 5G/6G reference signals (RSs). We show that the velocity profile induced by such irregular time-domain patterns can be decomposed into a periodic-peak component and an amplitude-shaping (weighting) component. Leveraging this structure, we propose a multi-periodogram velocity estimation algorithm that is standard-compliant and does not require new sensing-dedicated RSs or 3GPP modifications. Simulation results demonstrate that, compared with conventional periodogram processing, the proposed method improves low-SNR robustness by achieving a 3 dB SNR gain at the 10% missed-detection rate and reducing false alarms by 51%.

</details>


### [141] [FlowS: One-Step Motion Prediction via Local Transport Conditioning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.26065)
*Leandro Di Bella,Adrian Munteanu,Bruno Cornelis*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 本文提出FlowS框架，通过局部传输条件化（local transport conditioning）策略，使扩散模型能够在单步推理中同时实现高精度、多模态未来预测和严格低延迟，在Waymo数据集上达到SOTA性能（Soft mAP 0.4804，mAP 0.4703，75 FPS）。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 生成式运动预测需要同时满足高精度、多模态未来和严格低延迟三个要求。扩散模型能满足前两个要求，但需要数十到数百步去噪步骤，违反了低延迟要求。作者旨在解决这一矛盾，使单步生成式运动预测成为可能。

Method: 提出局部传输条件化（local transport conditioning）策略，核心思想：当底层传输问题是局部时，单步积分是准确的。具体实现为FlowS框架，包含两个机制：(1) 在线场景条件学习先验，为每个智能体发射K个校准锚点轨迹（已接近合理未来），将模式发现转化为局部校正；(2) 步一致位移场（step-consistent displacement field），强制执行半群自一致性，保证单步继承多步精度。在直线路径的学得先验上锚定该字段，获得稳定低方差训练目标。

Result: 在Waymo开放运动数据集上，FlowS以单步推理实现SOTA性能：Soft mAP 0.4804，mAP 0.4703，推理速度75 FPS（集成模型）。

Conclusion: 局部传输条件化使单步生成式运动预测在安全关键型自主系统中变得实用，成功解决了扩散模型高精度与低延迟之间的矛盾。

Abstract: Generative motion prediction must satisfy three simultaneous requirements for real-world autonomy: high accuracy, diverse multimodal futures, and strictly bounded latency. Diffusion models meet the first two but violate the third, requiring tens to hundreds of denoising steps. We identify a conditioning strategy that resolves this tension: \textit{single-step integration is accurate when the underlying transport problem is local}. A model that must both discover the correct behavioral mode and traverse a long displacement in one step accumulates large discretization errors; conditioning the base distribution to lie near plausible futures reduces the problem to short-range refinement, the regime where a single Euler step suffices. We instantiate this \emph{local transport conditioning} in FlowS, a conditional flow matching framework with two mechanisms. First, an online, scene-conditioned learned prior emits $K$ calibrated anchor trajectories per agent, each already near a plausible future, converting mode discovery into local correction. Second, a step-consistent displacement field enforces semigroup self-consistency, guaranteeing that a single step inherits multi-step accuracy. Crucially, anchoring this field at learned priors along straight-line paths yields a {stable, low-variance} training target, unlike prior self-consistency methods that suffer from {high-variance bootstrap} signals on curved diffusion paths. On the Waymo Open Motion Dataset, FlowS achieves state-of-the-art Soft mAP {(0.4804) and mAP (0.4703) with ensemble at 75\,FPS} with single-step inference, demonstrating that local transport conditioning makes one-step generative motion prediction practical for safety-critical autonomy. Code and pretrained models will be released upon acceptance.

</details>


### [142] [Lights Out: A Nighttime UAV Localization Framework Using Thermal Imagery and Semantic 3D Maps](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.26201)
*Ryan Allen,Melissa Greeff*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 本文提出一种语义重投影框架，通过将夜间热成像分割结果与白天RGB构建的语义3D地图对齐，实现无人机在GNSS拒止夜间的可靠定位，在6.5km真实轨迹上达到1.52m中位RMSE。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 解决无人机在GNSS拒止夜间环境下难以利用热成像与白天RGB地图进行定位的问题，核心挑战在于两种模态间的巨大外观差异。

Method: 构建共享语义域，将定位问题形式化为对称双向重投影目标函数，并引入混淆感知权重以提升分割不确定性下的鲁棒性。

Result: 在6.5km真实夜间无人机飞行轨迹上，偏差校正后RMSE2D为2.18m，中位RMSE2D为1.52m；定位性能与语义边缘证据可用性高度相关，大误差事件集中于语义模糊区域。

Conclusion: 语义重投影是仅使用热成像实现全局参考系夜间无人机定位的有效途径。

Abstract: Reliable backup localization for unmanned aerial vehicles (UAVs) operating in GNSS-denied nighttime conditions remains an open challenge due to the severe modality gap between daytime RGB maps and nighttime thermal imagery. This work presents a semantic reprojection framework for map-relative nighttime UAV localization by aligning segmented thermal observations with a globally referenced, semantically labeled 3D map constructed from daytime RGB data. Rather than relying on appearance-based correspondence, localization is formulated in a shared semantic domain and solved via a symmetric bidirectional reprojection objective with confusion-aware weighting to improve robustness under segmentation uncertainty. The approach is evaluated offline across 6.5 km of nighttime, real-world UAV flight trajectories in urban and semi-structured environments. Relative to RTK GNSS ground truth, the system achieves a bias-corrected RMSE2D of 2.18 m and a median RMSE2D of 1.52 m. Results show that localization performance is strongly correlated with the availability of semantic edge evidence and that large-error events are spatially localized to semantically ambiguous areas rather than uniformly distributed. These findings indicate that semantic reprojection offers a promising pathway toward globally referenced nighttime UAV localization using thermal imagery alone.

</details>


### [143] [2D and 3D Grasp Planners for the GET Asymmetrical Gripper](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.26212)
*Andrew Goldberg,Ethan Ransing,Anton Kourakin,Cael Magner,Edward H. Adelson,Ken Goldberg*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 本文提出两种针对GET非对称夹爪的抓取规划器：基于单视角RGB-D图像的快速规划器GET-2D-1.0（使用Ferrari-Canny度量和新型采样策略）和基于网格的GET-3D-1.0（使用3D夹爪模型和光线追踪）。物理实验表明，GET-2D-1.0在提升成功率、抗抖动和抗阻力方面比基线提升超40%；GET-3D-1.0在提升成功率和抗抖动方面略有改进，但计算成本更高（平均17秒 vs 683毫秒）。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 针对GET非对称夹爪设计高效的抓取规划器，解决现有方法在计算效率和抓取质量上的不足。

Method: 提出两种方法：GET-2D-1.0基于单视角RGB-D图像，采用Ferrari-Canny度量与新型采样策略实现快速抓取规划；GET-3D-1.0基于3D夹爪网格模型和光线追踪技术进行更精确的抓取规划。

Result: GET-2D-1.0在提升成功率、抗抖动和抗阻力上比包围盒基线提升超40%。GET-3D-1.0在提升成功率和抗抖动上略优于GET-2D-1.0，但规划时间长达17秒，远高于GET-2D-1.0的683毫秒。

Conclusion: GET-2D-1.0在效率与性能之间取得良好平衡，是实际应用中的更优选择；GET-3D-1.0虽精度略高但计算代价过大。

Abstract: In this paper, we introduce GET-2D-1.0, a fast grasp planner for the GET asymmetrical gripper that operates from a single-view RGB-D image, using the Ferrari-Canny metric and a novel sampling strategy, and GET-3D-1.0, a mesh-based method using a 3D gripper model and ray-tracing. We evaluate both grasp planners against baselines with physical experiments, which suggest that GET-2D-1.0 can improve over a bounding box baseline by over 40% in lift success, shake survival, and force resistance. Experiments with GET-3D-1.0 suggest slight improvement compared to GET-2D-1.0 on lift success and shake survival, but are more computationally expensive, averaging 17 seconds of planning compared to 683 ms for GET-2D-1.0.

</details>


### [144] [Split over $n$ resource sharing problem: Are fewer capable agents better than many simpler ones?](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.26374)
*Karthik Soma,Mohamed S. Talamali,Genki Miyauchi,Giovanni Beltrame,Heiko Hamann,Roderich Gross*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 本文研究多智能体系统中有限资源应集中还是分散的问题，通过形式化分析资源分割问题，发现资源分割策略的效果取决于速度与半径/覆盖面积的缩放关系，并指出资源分割会增加个体失效概率。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 在多智能体系统中，有限资源（如预算、计算资源、物理尺寸）应该集中在少数能力强的智能体上，还是分散给多个简单智能体？这是资源分配的基本权衡问题。

Method: 形式化定义了n个智能体等额共享公共资源的资源分割问题，以多智能体覆盖作为案例研究，其中盘形覆盖面积随1/n缩放。进行了理论分析和计算机仿真验证。

Result: 初始覆盖率随n增加而增长；若智能体速度与其半径成比例减小，则不同规模组表现相同；若速度与覆盖面积成比例减小，则单个智能体表现最佳；资源分割会增加个体失效概率。

Conclusion: 该模型和发现有助于识别最优分散程度，并指导资源约束下多智能体系统的设计。

Abstract: In multi-agent systems, should limited resources be concentrated into a few capable agents or distributed among many simpler ones? This work formulates the split over $n$ resource sharing problem where a group of $n$ agents equally shares a common resource (e.g., monetary budget, computational resources, physical size). We present a case study in multi-agent coverage where the area of the disk-shaped footprint of agents scales as $1/n$. A formal analysis reveals that the initial coverage rate grows with $n$. However, if the speed of agents decreases proportionally with their radii, groups of all sizes perform equally well, whereas if it decreases proportionally with their footprints, a single agent performs best. We also present computer simulations in which resource splitting increases the failure rates of individual agents. The models and findings help identify optimal distributiveness levels and inform the design of multi-agent systems under resource constraints.

</details>


### [145] [Reactive Motion Generation via Phase-varying Neural Potential Functions](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.26450)
*Ahmet Tekden,Dimitrios Kanoulas,Aude Billard,Yasemin Bekiroglu*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: PNPF提出了一种基于相位变量的神经势函数框架，通过从状态进展中估计相位而非开环时间输入，有效处理轨迹交叉点并兼具稳定性与鲁棒性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有动态系统方法在处理轨迹交叉点（如画"8"字）时存在局限：二阶动态系统对扰动敏感，相位变量方法则因依赖开环时间/相位变量而难以从扰动中恢复。

Method: 提出Phase-varying Neural Potential Functions (PNPF)，将势函数条件于一个由状态进展直接估计的相位变量上，而非开环时间输入。该相位变量允许系统处理状态重访，同时学习的势函数生成局部向量场以实现反应式稳定控制。

Result: PNPF在点对点、周期和全6D运动任务中均有效泛化，在带交叉点的轨迹上优于现有基线方法，并在外部扰动下的实时机器人操作中展现出鲁棒性能。

Conclusion: PNPF通过状态驱动的相位变量与神经势函数的结合，为存在交叉点的复杂运动技能学习提供了兼具稳定性与鲁棒性的解决方案。

Abstract: Dynamical systems (DS) methods for Learning-from-Demonstration (LfD) provide stable, continuous policies from few demonstrations. First-order dynamical systems (DS) are effective for many point-to-point and periodic tasks, as long as a unique velocity is defined for each state. For tasks with intersections (e.g., drawing an "8"), extensions such as second-order dynamics or phase variables are often used. However, by incorporating velocity, second-order models become sensitive to disturbances near intersections, as velocity is used to disambiguate motion direction. Moreover, this disambiguation may fail when nearly identical position-velocity pairs correspond to different onward motions. In contrast, phase-based methods rely on open-loop time or phase variables, which limit their ability to recover after perturbations. We introduce Phase-varying Neural Potential Functions (PNPF), an LfD framework that conditions a potential function on a phase variable which is estimated directly from state progression, rather than on open-loop temporal inputs. This phase variable allows the system to handle state revisits, while the learned potential function generates local vector fields for reactive and stable control. PNPF generalizes effectively across point-to-point, periodic, and full 6D motion tasks, outperforms existing baselines on trajectories with intersections, and demonstrates robust performance in real-time robotic manipulation under external disturbances.

</details>


### [146] [Alter-Art: Exploring Embodied Artistic Creation through a Robot Avatar](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.26473)
*Do Won Park,Samuele Bordini,Giorgio Grioli,Manuel G. Catalano,Antonio Bicchi*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 本文提出Alter-Art范式，通过远程操控机器人化身Alter-Ego，让艺术家以第一人称视角"成为"机器人进行绘画、舞蹈、戏剧等实体艺术创作，探索具身化对创作主体性、身份认同和环境交互的影响。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有机器人艺术框架将机器人视为自主创作者或协作工具，在人与机器之间留下了鸿沟；作者希望填补这一空白，让艺术家能够通过化身机器人摆脱物理限制，重塑创作身份，同时保持艺术创作扎根于物质世界。

Method: 开发机器人化身Alter-Ego，集成沉浸式遥操作与柔性驱动技术；在舞蹈、戏剧和绘画三个艺术领域开展实验，通过定性艺术反馈分析具身化对创作主体性、身份认同和环境交互的影响。

Result: 艺术家能快速在机器人身体中建立临场感；机器人的物理约束会对创作过程产生影响，且在不同艺术领域表现各异。

Conclusion: 具身化应作为社会机器人学和远程呈现艺术表达的核心设计原则，可为无障碍艺术创作提供新的可能性。

Abstract: As with every emerging technology, new tools in the hands of artists reshape the nature of artwork creation. Current frameworks for robotics in arts deploy the robot as an autonomous creator or a collaborator, thus leaving a certain gap between the human artist and the machine. Now, we stand at the dawn of an era where artists can escape physical limitations and reshape their creative identity by inhabiting an alternative body. This new paradigm allows artists not only to command a robot remotely, but also to {\it be} a robot, to see and feel through it, experiencing a new embodied reality. Unlike virtual reality, where art is created in a digital dimension, in this case art creation is still firmly grounded in the material world: clay molded by mechanical hands, paint swept across a canvas or gestures performed on a physical stage alongside human actors. Through the robot avatar Alter-Ego, we explore the Alter-Art paradigm in dance, theater, and painting; it integrates immersive teleoperation and compliant actuation to enable a first-person creative experience. Analyzing qualitative artistic feedback, we investigate how embodiment shapes creative agency, identity and interaction with the environment. Our findings suggest that artists rapidly develop a sense of presence within the robotic body. The robot's physical constraints influence the creative process, manifesting differently across artistic domains. We highlight embodiment as a central design principle, contributing to social robotics and expanding the possibilities for telepresence and accessible artistic expression.

</details>


### [147] [HiPAN: Hierarchical Posture-Adaptive Navigation for Quadruped Robots in Unstructured 3D Environments](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.26504)
*Jeil Jeong,Minsung Yoon,Seokryun Choi,Heechan Shin,Taegeun Yang,Sung-eui Yoon*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 本文提出HiPAN框架，通过层次化设计（高层策略生成导航指令，低层控制器执行姿态自适应运动）和路径引导的课程学习，在非结构化3D环境中实现对四足机器人的高效导航，相比传统反应式规划器和端到端基线方法在成功率和路径效率上表现更优。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 四足机器人在非结构化3D环境中导航面临三大挑战：目标导向运动、逃离局部极小值的有效探索、以及穿越狭窄低矮空间时的姿态适应。传统顺序建图-规划流水线存在累积感知误差和高计算开销的问题，限制了其在资源受限平台上的部署。

Method: 提出HiPAN框架，采用层次化设计：（1）高层策略生成平面速度和身体姿态等导航指令；（2）低层姿态自适应运动控制器执行指令。引入路径引导的课程学习，从反应式避障逐步扩展到策略性长程导航。

Result: 在仿真中，HiPAN相比经典反应式规划器和端到端基线方法取得了更高的导航成功率和更好的路径效率；真实世界实验验证了其在多种非结构化3D环境中的适用性。

Conclusion: HiPAN通过层次化架构和课程学习有效解决了四足机器人在复杂3D环境中的导航难题，在降低计算开销的同时提升了导航性能，具有良好的实际部署潜力。

Abstract: Navigating quadruped robots in unstructured 3D environments poses significant challenges, requiring goal-directed motion, effective exploration to escape from local minima, and posture adaptation to traverse narrow, height-constrained spaces. Conventional approaches employ a sequential mapping-planning pipeline but suffer from accumulated perception errors and high computational overhead, restricting their applicability on resource-constrained platforms. To address these challenges, we propose Hierarchical Posture-Adaptive Navigation (HiPAN), a framework that operates directly on onboard depth images at deployment. HiPAN adopts a hierarchical design: a high-level policy generates strategic navigation commands (planar velocity and body posture), which are executed by a low-level, posture-adaptive locomotion controller. To mitigate myopic behaviors and facilitate long-horizon navigation, we introduce Path-Guided Curriculum Learning, which progressively extends the navigation horizon from reactive obstacle avoidance to strategic navigation. In simulation, HiPAN achieves higher navigation success rates and greater path efficiency than classical reactive planners and end-to-end baselines, while real-world experiments further validate its applicability across diverse, unstructured 3D environments.

</details>


### [148] [3D Generation for Embodied AI and Robotic Simulation: A Survey](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.26509)
*Tianwei Ye,Yifan Mao,Minwen Liao,Jian Liu,Chunchao Guo,Dazhao Du,Quanxin Shou,Fangqi Zhu,Song Guo*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 本文首次系统综述了面向具身AI的3D生成技术，围绕数据生成器、仿真环境和Sim2Real桥接三个角色组织文献，指出该领域正从视觉真实感向交互就绪性转变，并识别了物理标注不足、几何质量与物理有效性脱节、评估碎片化及Sim2Real鸿沟等关键瓶颈。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 具身AI和机器人系统越来越依赖可扩展、多样且物理真实的3D内容，但现有3D生成主要关注视觉真实感，缺乏面向交互和任务执行所需的运动学结构、材质属性和场景支持，亟需系统梳理该领域的研究现状与挑战。

Method: 采用综述方法，将3D生成在具身AI中的角色划分为三类：数据生成器（生成可交互的刚体、铰接体、可变形物体等仿真资产）、仿真环境（构建结构感知、可控、具智能体的交互式场景）、Sim2Real桥接（支持数字孪生重建、数据增强和合成演示），并基于此框架对文献进行组织与分类。

Result: 揭示了该领域从视觉真实感向交互就绪性的范式转变趋势，识别出四个主要瓶颈：物理标注数据稀缺、几何质量与物理有效性之间的差距、零散且不统一的评估体系、以及Sim2Real迁移的持续挑战。

Conclusion: 3D生成要成为具身智能的可靠基础，必须解决物理标注、几何-物理一致性、统一评估和Sim2Real迁移等核心瓶颈。

Abstract: Embodied AI and robotic systems increasingly depend on scalable, diverse, and physically grounded 3D content for simulation-based training and real-world deployment. While 3D generative modeling has advanced rapidly, embodied applications impose requirements far beyond visual realism: generated objects must carry kinematic structure and material properties, scenes must support interaction and task execution, and the resulting content must bridge the gap between simulation and reality. This survey presents the first survey of 3D generation for embodied AI and organizes the literature around three roles that 3D generation plays in embodied systems. In \emph{Data Generator}, 3D generation produces simulation-ready objects and assets, including articulated, physically grounded, and deformable content for downstream interaction; in \emph{Simulation Environments}, it constructs interactive and task-oriented worlds, spanning structure-aware, controllable, and agentic scene generation; and in \emph{Sim2Real Bridge}, it supports digital twin reconstruction, data augmentation, and synthetic demonstrations for downstream robot learning and real-world transfer. We also show that the field is shifting from visual realism toward interaction readiness, and we identify the main bottlenecks, including limited physical annotations, the gap between geometric quality and physical validity, fragmented evaluation, and the persistent sim-to-real divide, that must be addressed for 3D generation to become a dependable foundation for embodied intelligence. Our project page is at https://3dgen4robot.github.io.

</details>


### [149] [LLM-Flax : Generalizable Robotic Task Planning via Neuro-Symbolic Approaches with Large Language Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.26569)
*Seongmin Kim,Daegyu Lee*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: LLM-Flax 是一个三阶段框架，利用本地部署的大语言模型（LLM），仅需PDDL领域文件即可自动完成神经符号任务规划器的部署，无需人工编写规则、训练数据或训练GNN。在MazeNamo基准测试中，平均成功率0.945，超越人工基线0.828。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 当前神经符号任务规划器部署到新领域需要大量人工：领域专家需手动编写松弛规则和互补规则，且需要解决数百个训练问题来监督GNN对象评分器。这导致部署成本高、可扩展性差。

Method: 提出三阶段框架LLM-Flax：阶段1通过结构化提示、格式验证和自我纠错自动生成松弛规则和互补规则；阶段2引入LLM引导的故障恢复机制，采用可行性门控预算策略，在每次LLM调用前预留API延迟成本，避免下游松弛回退资源枯竭；阶段3用零样本LLM对象重要性评分替代领域训练的GNN，无需训练数据。

Result: 在MazeNamo基准测试的8个基准（10x10、12x12、15x15网格）上，LLM-Flax平均SR 0.945，人工基线0.828（+0.117）。12x12 Expert上SR 0.733（人工完全失败，SR 0.000）；15x15 Hard上SR 1.000（人工0.900）。阶段3在12x12 Hard上以零训练数据达到SR 0.720。

Conclusion: LLM-Flax成功消除了神经符号规划器部署中三项主要人工成本，性能全面超越人工基线。阶段3展示了零训练数据部署的可行性，但面临上下文窗口瓶颈，这是未来工作的主要开放挑战。

Abstract: Deploying a neuro-symbolic task planner on a new domain today requires significant manual effort: a domain expert must author relaxation and complementary rules, and hundreds of training problems must be solved to supervise a Graph Neural Network (GNN) object scorer. We propose LLM-Flax, a three-stage framework that eliminates all three sources of manual effort using a locally hosted LLM given only a PDDL domain file. Stage 1 automatically generates relaxation and complementary rules via structured prompting with format validation and self-correction. Stage 2 introduces LLM-guided failure recovery with a feasibility-gated budget policy that explicitly reserves API latency cost before each LLM call, preventing the downstream relaxation fallback from being starved. Stage 3 replaces the domain-trained GNN entirely with zero-shot LLM object importance scoring, requiring no training data. We evaluate all three stages on the MazeNamo benchmark across 10x10, 12x12, and 15x15 grids (8 benchmarks total). LLM-Flax achieves average SR 0.945 versus the manual baseline's 0.828 (+0.117), matching or outperforming manual rules on every one of the eight benchmarks. On 12x12 Expert, LLM-Flax attains SR 0.733 where the manual planner fails entirely (SR 0.000); on 15x15 Hard, it achieves SR 1.000 versus Manual's 0.900. Stage 3 demonstrates feasibility (SR 0.720 on 12x12 Hard with no training data) but faces a context-window bottleneck at scale, pointing to the primary open challenge for future work.

</details>


### [150] [STAR-Filter: Efficient Convex Free-Space Approximation via Starshaped Set Filtering in Noisy Environments](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.26626)
*Yuwei Wu,Yichen Zhao,Dexter Ong,Vijay Kumar*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 提出STAR-Filter轻量级框架，利用星形集构造作为快速滤波器生成凸碰撞自由空间，显著降低计算冗余并提升对传感器噪声的鲁棒性，在真实噪声和大规模数据上实现了最低计算时间和更低保守性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有基于迭代优化的凸区域膨胀方法在复杂障碍环境和噪声传感器数据下效率严重下降，且对初始化和精确几何模型敏感，亟需一种轻量、鲁棒的碰撞自由空间近似方法。

Method: 提出STAR-Filter框架，通过星形集（starshaped set）构造作为快速滤波器，识别障碍物点作为主动支撑约束，在保证可行性的同时大幅减少冗余计算，并具备对传感器噪声的鲁棒性。
